		next;

		mes "Voce e provavelmente o apenas one who nao pode tell o difference entre him e me.";

	}
	mes "[Masked Person]";
	mes "Freyum is amor.";

OnTouch:
	.@char_id = getcharid(0);
	if (ep18_main == 0) {

		unittalk getcharid(3), "" + strcharinfo(0) + " : Mini Elly: Please take it out of the bag and put it on top of my head.", bc_self;
		close;

	if (ep18_main == 2 || ep18_main == 3) {
		cloakoffnpcself( "Crowd#ep18c01" );
		cloakoffnpcself( "Crowd#ep18c04" );
		cloakoffnpcself( "Crowd#ep18c05" );

wolfvill,53,234,4	script	Transport Cart#ep18_1	4_EP18_WAGON,{
	if (ep18_main < 36)
		end;
	if (isbegin_quest(12532) == 1) {
		mes "^0000ffAlthough it looks fine, hum um worn-out carriage wheel..^000000";
		next;
		if (select( "I attempt maintenance trabalhar", "I parar o maintenance trabalhar" ) == 2) {
			mes "^0000ffVamos revisit este when it comes to mente later.^000000";
			close;
		}
		progressbar_npc "FFFF00",2;
		if (countitem(7197) < 1) {
			mes "Materials suitable fou reinforcing o carriage wheel <ITEM>[Tough Vines]<INFO>7197</INFO></ITEM> unavailable.";
			close;
		}
		// note: Tough_Vines not deleted on fail
		.@r = rand(1,5);
		if (.@r == 1) {
			specialeffect EF_REFINEOK;
			mes "^0000ffRepair successful!^000000";
			delitem 7197,1;	// Tough_Vines
			removespecialeffect 1090;
			completequest 12532;
			close;
		}
		specialeffect EF_REFINEFAIL;
		mes "^0000ffIt nao parecer to tem sido properly reinforced yet. O material nao tem disappeared, entao vamos manter tentando^000000";
		close;
	}
	end;

OnInit:
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST2, QMARK_YELLOW, "isbegin_quest(12532) == 1" );
	end;
}

gw_fild01,205,102,4	script(CLOAKED)	#ep18_2_mon	EP18_GREY_WOLF,{
	if (ep18_main < 36)
		end;
	if (isbegin_quest(12534) != 1)
		end;
	.@wolf_hp = 1000;
	.@camille_hp = 1000;

	mes "^0000ffO situation nao parecer favorable.^000000";
	next;
	npctalk "Arrgghh...", "#ep18_2_mon", bc_self;
	next;
	npctalk "Good grief", "Camille#ep18_2", bc_self;
	next;
	mes "^0000ffSeems like Eu sou sobre to collapse. Uncapaz de focus on where to atacar, meu olhos are rolling sem restraint. Provavelmente nao vai ultimo ate mesmo um second, se tornando um facil meal fou o wolves^000000";
	next;
	while(1) {
		next;
		switch( select( "Dar combat advice to Camille", "I handle o wolves personally", "Pretend nao to aviso e pass by" ) ) {
		case 1:
			mes "^0000ffTell Camille where to atacar o wolves?^000000";
			PlayBGM "182";
			while(1) {
				mes "Wolf vitality - ^ff0000" + .@wolf_hp + "^000000";
				mes "Camille's vitality - ^0000ff" + .@camille_hp + "^000000";
				next;
				switch( select( "Timid atacar(High probability)", "Moderate atacar(Normal probability)", "Forte atacar(Low probability)", "Counterattack(Thin Probability)", "Correr emborum fou agora" ) ) {
				case 1:
					.@wolf_chance = 65;	// 65% chance the wolf is being hit / 35% Camille
					.@wolf_damage = rand(25,50);
					.@camille_damage = rand(45,93);
					break;
				case 2:
					.@wolf_chance = 42;
					.@wolf_damage = rand(32,58);
					.@camille_damage = rand(37,72);
					break;
				case 3:
					.@wolf_chance = 30;
					.@wolf_damage = rand(45,70);
					.@camille_damage = rand(34,53);
					break;
				case 4:
					.@wolf_chance = 25;
					.@wolf_damage = rand(66,80);
					.@camille_damage = rand(26,50);
					break;
				case 5:
					mes "Eu sou chateado, mas hum no chance of vencendo agora. Vamos consertar it e tentar novamente";
					close;
				}
				if (.@wolf_chance > rand(100)) {
					.@wolf_hp = .@wolf_hp - .@wolf_damage;
					npctalk "-" + .@wolf_damage, "#ep18_2_mon", bc_self;
					npctalk " ", "Camille#ep18_2", bc_self;
					specialeffect EF_ACIDDEMON, SELF, "#ep18_2_mon";
					specialeffect EF_HFLIMOON3, SELF, "#ep18_2_mon";
				}
				else {
					.@camille_hp = .@camille_hp - .@camille_damage;
					npctalk " ", "#ep18_2_mon", bc_self;
					npctalk "-" + .@camille_damage, "Camille#ep18_2", bc_self;
					specialeffect EF_ACIDDEMON, SELF, "Camille#ep18_2";
					specialeffect EF_HFLIMOON3, SELF, "Camille#ep18_2";
				}
				if (.@wolf_hp < 1) {
					mes "^0000ffVoce tem defeated o gray wolf.^000000";
					completequest 12534;
					cloakonnpcself( "#ep18_2_mon" );
					playbgm "183";
					close;
				}
				if (.@camille_hp < 1) {
					next;
					mes "^ff0000Dangerous!! Eu precientao de to parar o batalhum e restore Camille's health.^000000";
					playbgm "183";
					close;
				}
			}
			break;
		case 2:
			mes "^0000ff No, it pode ser certo to apenas ajudar Camille grow up on a own....^000000";
			continue;
		case 3:
			mes "^0000ffVolte later e construir Camille's grave.^000000";
			close;
		}
		end;
	}
	end;

OnInit:
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST2, QMARK_YELLOW, "isbegin_quest(12534) == 1" );
	end;
}

	if (checkweight(1000405,20) == 0) {
		mes "- Espere um minuto !! -";
		mes "- Currently voce e carregando -";
		mes "- tambem muitos itens com you. -";
		mes "- Pou favou tentar novamente -";
		mes "- depois you loose alguns weight. -";
		close;
	}
	if (ep18_main < 36)
		end;
	if (isbegin_quest(8688) == 1 || isbegin_quest(8690) == 1) {
		.@amount = countitem(1000412) + countitem(1000413) + countitem(1000414);
		if (.@amount >= 5) {
			mes "Eu tenho collected suficiente coisas caught in o traps hoje.";
			mes "Vamos trazer it to <NAVI>[Eruin]<INFO>wolfvill,202,166,0,101,0</INFO></NAVI>.";
			close;
		}
		specialeffect2 EF_STEAL;
		progressbar "FFFF00",10;
		.@r = rand(1,5);
		if (.@r == 1)
			getitem 1000412,1;	// Ep18_Trapped01
		else if (.@r == 2)
			getitem 1000413,1;	// Ep18_Trapped02
		else if (.@r == 3)
			getitem 1000414,1;	// Ep18_Trapped03
		else
			mes "I nao pegar anything...";
		disablenpc strnpcinfo(0);
		initnpctimer;
		end;
	}
	end;
OnTimer60000:
	enablenpc strnpcinfo(0);
	stopnpctimer;
	end;

OnInit:
	questinfo( QTYPE_CLICKME, QMARK_YELLOW, "isbegin_quest(8688) == 1" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_CLICKME, QMARK_YELLOW, "isbegin_quest(8690) == 1" );
	end;
}

	end;

	end;

	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "checkquest(16548,HUNTING) == -1 && isbegin_quest(11724) == 2" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "checkquest(16548,HUNTING) == 2" );

	end;

			}
			mes "[Junok]";
			mes "Entao, Eu vou sair it to you. Se voce precisum any assistance, feel gratis to ask. Have um otimo dia!.";
			next;
			mes "[Junok]";
			mes "Pou favou volte depois conversando to o 3 natives e o 3 immigrants.";
			next;
			mes "[Junok]";
			mes "O list of pessoas to conversar to cum be encontrou in o missao window ou on o mini-map.";
			close;
		}
		break;
	}
	switch( isbegin_quest(3505) ) {
	case 0:
		mes "[Junok]";
		mes "Pou favou have conversations com todos 6 citizens e retornar afterward.";
		close;
	case 1:	// task completed
		switch( checkquest(3504,PLAYTIME) ) {
		case -1:	// first completion
			mes "[Junok]";
			mes "Isentao e so...";
			next;
			mes "[Junok]";
			mes "It parece hum aindum um lot of resentment entre them.";
			next;
			mes "[Junok]";
			mes "E worse que I expected. Hm... I imaginar what cum be done sobre this.";
			next;
			getitem 1000405,10;	// Ep18_Amethyst_Fragment
			getexp 7769124,3000000;	// dummy value
			break;
		case 0:
		case 1:	// (shouldn't happen)
			mes "[Junok]";
			mes "Hoje, Eu tenho gathered suficiente informacao. Se voce precisum assistance novamente, pou favou volte amanha.";
			close;
		case 2:	// daily quest
			mes "[Junok]";
			mes "Isentao e so...";
			next;
			mes "[Junok]";
			mes "It parece hum aindum um lot of resentment entre them.";
			next;
			mes "[Junok]";
			mes "E worse que I expected. Hm... I imaginar what cum be done sobre this.";
			next;
			getitem 1000405,3;	// Ep18_Amethyst_Fragment
			getexp 18252408,2000000;
			break;
		}
		for ( .@i = 3493; .@i < 3506; ++.@i ) {
			if (isbegin_quest(.@i) > 0)
				erasequest .@i;
		}
		setquest 3504;
		mes "[Junok]";
		mes "Erum helpful guidance..";
		next;
		mes "[Junok]";
		mes "Obrigado fou seu ajudar. Pou favou volte novamente se voce pode assist amanha.";
		close;
	case 2:
		end;
	}
	end;

OnInit:
	questinfo( QTYPE_DAILYQUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "ep18_main >= 12 && isbegin_quest(3503) == 0 && (checkquest(3504,PLAYTIME) == -1 || checkquest(3504,PLAYTIME) == 2)" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "isbegin_quest(3505) == 1" );
	end;
}

rachel,176,80,0	script	#Hoesopflowerstart	HIDDEN_WARP_NPC,10,10,{
	end;
OnTouch:
	if (BaseLevel < 170)
		end;
	switch( isbegin_quest(5932) ) {
	case 0:
		cloakoffnpcself( "#Directing Warp" );
		cloakoffnpcself( "Greedy Man#E18_1" );
		cloakoffnpcself( "Abused Child#E18_2" );

		mes "- A mum who aparece to be greedy";
		mes "- Is yelling at um child.";
		next;
		if (select( "Ouvir to o que e happening.", "Ignore it." ) == 2)
			end;
		emotion ET_FRET, getnpcid(0,"Greedy Man#E18_1");
		npctalk "Man: Is this all I get in return after feeding and sheltering a tribal child with nowhere else to go?", "Greedy Man#E18_1", bc_self;
		emotion ET_SURPRISE, playerattached();
		unittalk getcharid(3), "" + strcharinfo(0) + " : ??", bc_self;
		next;
		emotion ET_ANGER, getnpcid(0,"Greedy Man#E18_1");
		npctalk "Man: Huh?! This isn't even funny. How dare you lay your hands on a guest's wallet?", "Greedy Man#E18_1", bc_self;
		next;
		emotion ET_FRET, getnpcid(0,"Greedy Man#E18_1");

// Quest: Picking Half Flowers. Step 3 (final).
rachel,72,133,4	script(CLOAKED)	Half Flower#E18_6	4_EP18_HALFFLOWER,{
	if (checkweight(1000407,1) == 0) {
		mes "- Espere um minuto !! -";
		mes "- Currently voce e carregando -";
		mes "- tambem muitos itens com you. -";
		mes "- Pou favou tentar novamente -";
		mes "- depois you loose alguns weight. -";
		close;
	}
	if (isbegin_quest(5935) == 1) {
		setarray .@good_answer[0],2,2,2,3;
		.@time = 86400;
		mes "Este is um practice Half Flower.";
		next;
		npctalk "Mejai: Let's assume that Half Flowers are blooming under the trees and start practicing.", "Mejai#E18_5", bc_self;
		next;
		npctalk "Mejai: Shall we start by cutting the stems of the Half Flowers?", "Mejai#E18_5", bc_self;
		next;
		if (select( "Cut at 10�� angle.", "Cut at 20�� angle.", "Cut at 30�� angle.", "Cut at 40�� angle." ) != .@good_answer[0])
			.@time /= 2;
		progressbar "FFFF00",1;
		npctalk "Mejai: Thank you for your decision. Let's start by cutting the stem first.", "Mejai#E18_5", bc_self;
		next;
		npctalk "Mejai: Understood. Next, I'll remove the leaves. I'll leave just enough and remove the rest.", "Mejai#E18_5", bc_self;
		next;
		if (select( "Sair 2 leaves.", "Sair 4 leaves.", "Sair 6 leaves.", "Sair 8 leaves." ) != .@good_answer[1])
			.@time /= 2;
		progressbar "FFFF00",1;
		npctalk "Mejai: Well done. Next, we'll prepare a jar to soak the stem end.", "Mejai#E18_5", bc_self;
		next;
		npctalk "Mejai: First, please fill the small jar with water.", "Mejai#E18_5", bc_self;
		next;
		if (select( "Pnosso 20 ml.", "Pnosso 30 ml.", "Pnosso 40 ml.", "Pnosso 50 ml." ) != .@good_answer[2])
			.@time /= 2;
		progressbar "FFFF00",1;
		npctalk "Mejai: I'll now add the secret powder I made to the water and mix it.", "Mejai#E18_5", bc_self;
		next;
		npctalk "Mejai: Please mix it in the right amount.", "Mejai#E18_5", bc_self;
		next;
		if (select( "Add 2 grams.", "Add 4 grams.", "Add 6 grams.", "Add 8 grams." ) != .@good_answer[3])
			.@time /= 2;
		npctalk "Mejai: Now, for the final step.", "Mejai#E18_5", bc_self;
		next;
		npctalk "Mejai: Now, just put the cut stem ends into the container with the water and secret powder we just mixed, and we're done with the packaging!", "Mejai#E18_5", bc_self;
		next;
		progressbar "FFFF00",1;
		// note: the timer decreases according to the answer but no idea of the ratio
		rentitem 1000407,.@time;	// Ep18_Half_Flower
		erasequest 5935;
		setquest 5936;
		cloakonnpcself( "Half Flower#E18_6" );
		npctalk "Mejai: Completed! Would you bring me the half flower you just harvested?", "Mejai#E18_5", bc_self;
		close;
	}
	end;

OnInit:
	setunittitle( getnpcid(0), "For Practice" );

	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "isbegin_quest(5935) == 1" );
	end;
}

		npctalk "Mejai: Oh, you're here? Shall we start today energetically as well?", "Mejai#E18_7", bc_self;
		mes "[Mejai]";
		mes "Bem-vindo. We were apenas sobre to hold um modest abrindo ceremony fou o 'Flower Room'.";
		next;
		mes "[Mejai]";
		mes "Hm...";
		next;
		mes "[Mejai]";
		mes "Emborum nao todos could attend este gathering, Eu sou truly grateful to esses who could join us e illuminate este occasion.";
		next;
		mes "[Mejai]";
		mes "I must conseguir aqui.";
		next;
		mes "[Mejai]";
		mes "Eu sei e nao ideal, mas este business was comecou com cadum ultimo penny I could reunir.";
		next;
		mes "[Mejai]";
		mes "Hum no segurancum net. So, Eu vou do meu best, e I esperancum you, meu employees, will do o mesmo.";
		next;
		mes "[Mejai]";
		mes "Eu vou strive to be um boss who repays seu best efforts com meu own.";
		next;
		setquest 5942;
		completequest 5942;
		mes "[Mejai]";
		mes "Gre abrindo sobre! Shall we agorum colocar nosso hearts parum dentro nosso trabalhar?";
		next;
	}
	switch( checkquest(5937,PLAYTIME) ) {
	case -1:
		break;
	case 0:
	case 1:
		setpcblock PCBLOCK_NPC, true;
		npctalk "Mejai: Come on! Today's work is done.", "Mejai#E18_7", bc_self;
		sleep2 1000;
		setpcblock PCBLOCK_NPC, false;
		npctalk "Mejai: Go home and be faithful to your family. It's not like this is the only thing you have to do.", "Mejai#E18_7", bc_self;
		end;
	case 2:
		setpcblock PCBLOCK_NPC, true;
		npctalk "Mejai: Hello. Let's do our best today too.", "Mejai#E18_7", bc_self;
		sleep2 1000;
		setpcblock PCBLOCK_NPC, false;
		break;
	}
	switch( select( "Eu sou currently ocupado..", "Harvesting Half Flowers", "Delivering Half Flowers" ) ) {
	case 1:
		npctalk "Mejai: It seems you're busy with something? Please come see me when you're free.", "Mejai#E18_7", bc_self;
		end;
	case 2:
		for ( .@id = 5938; .@id < 5942; .@id++ ) {
			if (isbegin_quest(.@id) == 1) {
				.@quest_id = .@id;
				break;
			}
		}
		if (.@quest_id == 0)
			.@quest_id = rand(5938,5941);

		switch( isbegin_quest(.@quest_id) ) {
		case 0:
			npctalk "Mejai: Are you planning to collect half flowers?", "Mejai#E18_7", bc_self;
			if (select( "Eu vou ir coletar them.", "Eu vou skip hoje." ) == 2)
				end;
			switch( checkquest(5937,PLAYTIME) ) {
			case -1:
				break;
			case 0:
			case 1:
				npctalk "Mejai: Oh! Are you sure?", "Mejai#E18_7", bc_self;
				setpcblock PCBLOCK_NPC, true;
				sleep2 1000;
				setpcblock PCBLOCK_NPC, false;
				npctalk "Mejai: Since today hasn't ended yet, if you happen to finish your tasks early today, please consider helping out at the Flower Room as well.", "Mejai#E18_7", bc_self;
				end;
			case 2:
				break;
			}
			setquest .@quest_id;
			npctalk "Mejai: Please be careful of the half flowers blooming near the trees today.", "Mejai#E18_7", bc_self;
			setpcblock PCBLOCK_NPC, true;
			sleep2 1000;
			setpcblock PCBLOCK_NPC, false;
			npctalk "Mejai: Alright! Fighting! Bring back some fresh half flowers with vigor today.", "Mejai#E18_7", bc_self;
			end;
		case 1:
			if (rentalcountitem(1000407) < 1) {
				npctalk "Mejai: Please continue your hard work for the fresh Half Flowers today.", "Mejai#E18_7", bc_self;
				end;
			}
			delitem 1000407,1;	// Eps18_Half_Flower
			erasequest .@quest_id;
			if (checkquest(5937,PLAYTIME) == 2)
				erasequest 5937;
			setquest 5937;
			getitem 1000405,3;	// Ep18_Amethyst_Fragment
			getexp 18252408,2000000;
			npctalk "Mejai: You've worked hard today as well. Please leave the rest to me.", "Mejai#E18_7", bc_self;
			end;
		case 2:
			end;	// impossible
		}
		end;
	case 3:
		npctalk "Mejai: It's a service that I'm still contemplating. Perhaps we could initiate it once the business stabilizes a bit?", "Mejai#E18_7", bc_self;
		setpcblock PCBLOCK_NPC, true;
		sleep2 1000;
		setpcblock PCBLOCK_NPC, false;
		npctalk "Mejai: When that time comes, I'll rely on your assistance.", "Mejai#E18_7", bc_self;
		end;
	}
	end;

OnInit:
	questinfo( QTYPE_DAILYQUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "isbegin_quest(5936) == 2 && isbegin_quest(5938) == 0 && isbegin_quest(5939) == 0 && isbegin_quest(5940) == 0 && isbegin_quest(5941) == 0 && checkquest(5937,PLAYTIME) == 2" );
	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "isbegin_quest(5936) == 2 && rentalcountitem(1000407) > 0" );
	end;
}

gw_fild01,325,376,1	script	Half Flower#EP18_R01	4_EP18_HALFFLOWER,7,7,{
	if (checkweight(1000407,1) == 0) {
		mes "- Espere um minuto !! -";
		mes "- Currently voce e carregando -";
		mes "- tambem muitos itens com you. -";
		mes "- Pou favou tentar novamente -";
		mes "- depois you loose alguns weight. -";
		close;
	}
	for ( .@id = 5938; .@id < 5942; .@id++ ) {
		if (isbegin_quest(.@id) == 1) {
			.@quest_id = .@id;
			break;
		}
	}
	if (.@quest_id == 0) {
		unittalk getcharid(3), "Half flower is blooming", bc_self;
		end;
	}
	// player interacts with the npc according to the quest ID
	// 5938 requires EP18_R (flower near a Rock)
	// 5939 requires EP18_L (?)
	// 5940 requires EP18_T (Tree)
	// 5941 requires EP18_W (Water)
	switch( .@quest_id ) {
	case 5938:
		.@string$ = "EP18_R";
		setarray .@good_answer[0],4,1,4,1;
		break;
	case 5939:
		.@string$ = "EP18_L";
		setarray .@good_answer[0],3,2,3,2;
		break;
	case 5940:
		.@string$ = "EP18_T";
		setarray .@good_answer[0],2,2,2,3;
		break;
	case 5941:
		.@string$ = "EP18_W";
		setarray .@good_answer[0],1,4,1,4;
		break;
	}
	if (compare(strnpcinfo(2), .@string$) == false) {
		cloakonnpc strnpcinfo(0), getcharid(0);
		end;
	}
	if (rentalcountitem(1000407) > 0) {
		mes "- o Half Flower is mais";
		mes "- nao necessario, antes wither";
		mes "- Trazer it to Mejai.";
		close;
	}
	.@time = 86400;
	mes "- O Half Flower is in bloom. Vamos";
	mes "- reunir o required materiais de the";
	mes "- harvesting tool provided by Mejai.";
	next;
	unittalk getcharid(3), "" + strcharinfo(0) + " : What angle should I cut the stem?", bc_self;
	next;
	if (select("Cut at 10�� angle", "Cut at 20�� angle", "Cut at 30�� angle", "Cut at 40�� angle" ) != .@good_answer[0])
		.@time /= 2;
	progressbar "FFFF00",1;
	unittalk getcharid(3), "" + strcharinfo(0) + " : How many leaves should I leave?", bc_self;
	next;
	if (select("Sair 2 leaves", "Sair 4 leaves", "Sair 6 leaves", "Sair 8 leaves" ) != .@good_answer[1])
		.@time /= 2;
	progressbar "FFFF00",1;
	unittalk getcharid(3), "" + strcharinfo(0) + " : How many milliliters of water should I put in the small bottle?", bc_self;
	next;
	if (select("Colocar in 20 ml", "Colocar in 30 ml", "Colocar in 40 ml", "Colocar in 50 ml" ) != .@good_answer[2])
		.@time /= 2;
	progressbar "FFFF00",1;
	unittalk getcharid(3), "" + strcharinfo(0) + " : How many grams of secret powder should I put in?", bc_self;
	next;
	if (select( "Colocar in 2 grams.", "Colocar in 4 grams.", "Colocar in 6 grams.", "Colocar in 8 grams." ) != .@good_answer[3])
		.@time /= 2;
	progressbar "FFFF00",1;
	cloakonnpcself();
	// note: the timer decreases according to the answer but no idea of the ratio
	rentitem 1000407, .@time;	// Ep18_Half_Flower
	mes "- Voce tem harvested o Half Flower.";
	mes "- Vamos entregar it to Mejai antes it withers.";
	close;

OnTouch:
	for ( .@id = 5938; .@id < 5942; .@id++ ) {
		if (isbegin_quest(.@id) == 1) {
			.@quest_id = .@id;
			break;
		}
	}
	if (.@quest_id == 0)
		end;
	setarray .@string$[5938], "EP18_R", "EP18_L", "EP18_T", "EP18_W";
	if (compare(strnpcinfo(2), .@string$[.@quest_id]) == false) {
		cloakonnpc strnpcinfo(0), getcharid(0);
		end;
	}
	end;
}

		next;
		mes "[Camper]";
		mes "Voce nao e assustado?";
		mes "O velho timers who perguntou me when Eu estavum vai pegar um job e everytime I meet them are caminho scarier.";
		mes "...";
		close;
	}
	if (checkweight(1000405,4) == 0) {
		mes "- Espere um minuto !! -";
		mes "- Currently voce e carregando -";
		mes "- tambem muitos itens com you. -";
		mes "- Pou favou tentar novamente -";
		mes "- depois you loose alguns weight. -";
		close;
	}
	if (checkquest(18086,HUNTING) == 2) {
		mes "[Gatekeeper]";
		mes "Oh, Eu ouvi you disse erum peaceful, entao you did meu favor.";

		mes "Vir on, Eu vou dar you what I have.";
		erasequest 18086;
		setquest 18087;
		add_reputation_points(REPUTATION_EP18, 30);
		if (get_reputation_points(REPUTATION_EP18) >= 5000)
			.@bonus = 1;
		if (get_reputation_points(REPUTATION_EP18) >= 1000 && ep18_main == 36) {// Custom implementation: reputation 1000 on wolfvill => player can continue the main quest
			ep18_main = 37;
			setquest 18082;
			mes "^4d4dff O villagers of o gray wolf have aceitou you as part of o vila.^000000";
			mes "Voce e eligible to participate in o ^4d4dff conference.^000000";

		getexp 9956480,2000000;
		next;
	}
	mes "[Gatekeeper]";
	mes "Voce e vai o vila?";
	next;
	switch( select( "To o Grey Wolf Vila", "Nao ir.", "Quem sao you?", "Fazer Grey Wolf Florestum safer" ) ) {
	case 1:
		mes "[Gatekeeper]";
		mes "Alright. Voce precisum to ir atraves o gap entre esses trees.";
		mes "Seguir me.";
		close2;
		warp "wolfvill",270,26;
		end;
	case 2:
		mes "[Gatekeeper]";
		mes "Okay entao. Que tal cacando alguns monstros nearby?";
		mes "They were tambem noisy when Eu estavum tentando to sleep.";
		close;
	case 3:
		mes "[Gatekeeper]";
		mes "As voce pode ver, Eu sou o gate keeper.";
		mes "I tambem drive emborum any unwanted visitors.";
		mes "Usually, I disguise myself as um vagabond, mas Eu sou nao num verdade one.";
		mes "Voce nao dare pensar of me as one!";
		close;
	case 4:
		if (ep18_main == 35) {
			mes "[Gatekeeper]";
			mes "No, no. I nao irium ask fou um favou certo depois encontrando you.";
			mes "O florestum pode ser um bit perigoso, mas e alright.";
			close;
		}
		switch( checkquest(18087,PLAYTIME) ) {
		case -1:
			break;
		case 0:
		case 1:
			mes "[Gatekeeper]";

		case 2:
			erasequest 18087;
			break;
		}
		switch( checkquest(18086,HUNTING) ) {
		case -1:
			mes "[Gatekeeper]";
			mes "As voce pode ver, Eu sou guarding o entrance aqui.";
			mes "Sometimes monstros vir todos o caminho aqui e bota me ate mesmo though Eu sou cansado.";

	questinfo( QTYPE_QUEST, QMARK_YELLOW, "checkquest(18086,HUNTING) == 2" );

gw_fild01,147,137,2	script	Boksil#wms04	EP18_GREY_WOLF_BABY,{
	end;
OnMove:
	while(1) {
		switch( .move ) {
		case 0:
			.val = !.val;
			setarray .@a[0],147,137;
			setarray .@b[0],147,138;
			break;
		case 1:
			setarray .@a[0],148,127;
			setarray .@b[0],147,127;
			break;
		case 2:
			setarray .@a[0],159,115;
			setarray .@b[0],160,115;
			break;
		case 3:
			setarray .@a[0],159,104;
			setarray .@b[0],160,104;
			break;
		case 4:
			setarray .@a[0],159,93;
			setarray .@b[0],160,93;
			break;
		case 5:
			setarray .@a[0],160,82;
			setarray .@b[0],161,82;
			break;
		case 6:
			.val = !.val;
			setarray .@a[0],161,71;
			setarray .@b[0],162,71;
			break;
		}
		.move = .move + .sens[.val];
		unitstopwalk getnpcid(0),USW_FORCE_STOP;
		unitstopwalk getnpcid(0,"Goat#wms03"),USW_FORCE_STOP;
		unitwalk getnpcid(0),.@a[0],.@a[1];
		unitwalk getnpcid(0,"Goat#wms03"),.@b[0],.@b[1];
		sleep 6000;
	}
	end;
OnInit:
	npcspeed 300;
	setarray .sens[0],1,-1;
	.move = 1;
	donpcevent strnpcinfo(0) + "::OnMove";
	end;
}

// Storage and unlock reputation feature
wolfvill,167,135,3	script	Asad#asad	4_EP18_GW_MAN01,{
	if (checkweight(1201,1) == 0) {
		mes "- Espere um minuto !! -";
		mes "- Currently voce e carregando -";
		mes "- tambem muitos itens com you. -";
		mes "- Pou favou tentar novamente -";
		mes "- depois you loose alguns weight. -";
		close;
	}
	.@reputation = get_reputation_points(REPUTATION_EP18);
	if (.@reputation < 1000) {
		mes "[Asad]";
		mes "Sir, e been um while desde Eu tenho visto you.";
		mes "Once you pegar somewhat familiar com o vila, vir encontrar me.";
		mes "Eu vou offer you alguns conveniences.";
		next;
		mes "[Asad]";
		mes "Mas that's apenas se voce e ^4d4dffvai participate in o tournament^000000.";
		mes "Eu nao apenas do favors fou anyone.";
		next;
		mes "[Asad]";
		mes "Se voce parece closely, lum sera places where um ajudando he is needed.";
		mes "By lending um mao, voce vai earn alguns goodwill among o pessoas, I reckon.";
		mes "Dar it seu best shot.";
		close;
	}

	if (.@reputation >= 3000) {
		mes "[Asad]";
		mes "Bem-vindo, meu wolf-like " + strcharinfo(0) + ".";
		mes "O que you precisar?";
		mes "Oh, Eu tenho been procurando fou um better smelting box.";
		mes "Voce gostarium de like to exchange it fou amethyst?";
	}
	else {
		mes "[Asad]";
		mes "Bem-vindo, Sir.";
		mes "Voce gostarium de like to tidy up alguns coisas?";
		mes "Ou voce gostarium de like to browse atraves alguns itens Eu tenho fou sale?";
	}
	next;
	.@amethyst_name$ = getitemname(1000405);
	if (.@reputation >= 4000)
		.@menu$[2] = "Exchange Smelting Box for " + .@amethyst_name$;
	else if (.@reputation >= 3000)
		.@menu$[1] = "Exchange Smelting Box for " + .@amethyst_name$;
	if (.@reputation >= 2000)
		.@menu$[0] = "Request space movement.";
	switch( select( "Usar o Armazem.", "Usar o store.", .@menu$[0], .@menu$[1], .@menu$[2] ) ) {
	case 1:
		mes "[Asad]";
		mes "O Armazem taxum is 200z.";
		mes "Which Armazem voce gostarium de like to abrir?";
		next;
		select( "Armazem 1" );
		if (Zeny < 200)
			end;
		Zeny -= 200;
		mes "[Asad]";
		mes "Alright, Eu vou abrir o Armazem.";
		mes "Usar it bem.";
		close2;
		openstorage();
		end;
	case 2:
		mes "[Asad]";
		mes "Voce e vai vender itens?";
		mes "Voce e vai comprar what you precisar?";
		next;
		switch( select( "Buy itens.", "Sell itens." ) ) {
		case 1:
			mes "[Asad]";
			mes "Eu sou nao sure se Eu tenho tudo you precisar.";
			mes "Mas whatever it is, voce pode count on me.";
			close2;
			callshop "Tool Dealer#wolf",1;
			end;
		case 2:
			mes "[Asad]";
			mes "Alright. Eu vou vender you anything at um reasonable preco.";
			close2;
			callshop "Tool Dealer#wolf",2;
			end;
		}
		end;
	case 3:
		mes "[Asad]";
		mes "Do voce quer to teleport to Rachel fou 1 " + .@amethyst_name$ + " ?";
		next;
		if (select( "Nao", "Sim" ) == 1)
			end;
		if (countitem(1000405) < 1) {
			mes "[Asad]";
			mes "Voce nao have suficiente material.";
			close;
		}
		delitem 1000405,1;	// Ep18_Amethyst_Fragment
		warp "rachel",115,125;
		end;
	case 4:
		mes "[Asad]";
		mes "Amethyst shop?";
		mes "Voce pode exchange them fou um smelting box..";
		close2;
		callshop "barter_ep18_2",1;
		end;
	case 5:
		mes "[Asad]";
		mes "Amethyst shop?";
		mes "Voce pode exchange them fou um smelting box..";
		close2;
		callshop "barter_ep18_3",1;
		end;
	}
	end;
}

		next;
		mes "[Emmet]";
		mes "Nos estamos coletando crystals.";
		mes "Voce gostarium de mente dando them to me later se you acontecer to reunir um large amount?";
		next;
		mes "[Emmet]";
		mes "E claro, I nao vai apenas pegar o crystal pieces fou nada.";
		mes "Eu vou exchange them fou equipamento ou outro itens esse aventureiros like yourself might precisar.";
		next;
		mes "[Emmet]";
		mes "When you reunir um large amount, pou favou vir to me.";
		mes "You entender, certo?";
		close;
	}
	disable_items;
	mes "[Emmet]";
	mes "Ola.";
	mes "Voce acontecer to have <ITEM>[Amethyst Fragment]<INFO>1000405</INFO></ITEM>?";
	mes "Nos estamos coletando crystals.";
	next;
	mes "[Emmet]";
	mes "We offer cuidadosamente crafted ^4d4dffarmou e enchantments^000000 in exchange fou crystals.";
	mes "What voce gostarium de like to do?";
	next;
	switch( select( "Exchange Armadura", "Gray Wolf Enchantment" ) ) {
	case 1:
		mes "[Emmet]";
		mes "As promised, voce tem trouxe muitos Amethyst Fragment?";
		mes "Pegar seu tempo e have um olhar ao redor.";
		close2;
		callshop "barter_ep18_1",1;
		end;
	case 2:
		mes "[Emmet]";
		mes "Voce have suficiente equipamento e materiais fou enchanting?";
		mes "I significa, o Amethyst Fragment.";
		mes "Pou favou select o part voce quer to enchant.";
		next;
		switch( select( "Armadura", "Garment", "Shoes", "Certo(R) Accessory", "Left(L) Accessory" ) ) {
		case 1:
			mes "[Emmet]";
			mes "Voce e enchanting o armadura, I ver.";
			close2;
			item_enchant(1);
			end;
		case 2:
			mes "[Emmet]";
			mes "Voce e enchanting o garment, I ver.";
			close2;
			item_enchant(2);
			end;
		case 3:
			mes "[Emmet]";
			mes "Voce e enchanting o shoes, I ver.";
			close2;
			item_enchant(3);
			end;
		case 4:
			mes "[Emmet]";
			mes "Voce e enchanting o certo accessory, I ver.";
			close2;
			item_enchant(4);
			end;
		case 5:
			mes "[Emmet]";
			mes "Voce e enchanting o left accessory, I ver.";
			close2;
			item_enchant(5);
			end;
		}
		end;
	}
	end;
}

wolfvill,170,131,3	script	Bamdat#bamdat	4_EP18_GW_OLD02,{
	if (ep18_main < 38) {
		mes "[Bamdatt]";

		mes "Agorum e dificil to encontrar any trabalhar...";
		mes "Mas se lum ever comes um tempo when meu habilidades are needed, Eu vou be pronto to ajudar.";
		close;
	}
	disable_items;
	mes "[Bamdatt]";
	mes "Eu ouvi o shrine's villum has mudou in um muito estranho caminho, certo?";
	mes "E where o fallen god passed by....";
	mes "O loot obtained de lum must tambem be filled com falentao beliefs.";
	next;
	mes "[Bamdatt]";
	mes "Se you wish, Eu consigo perform um purification ritual to transform them parum dentro armas filled com verdade faith.";
	mes "Meu sorcerous habilidades will tambem be colocar to bom usar.";
	next;
	mes "[Bamdatt]";
	mes "Voce tem um choice entre um basic purification ritual, which significantly reduces refining poder, e um advanced purification ritual, which maintains mais of o refining poder.";
	next;
	mes "Voce pode reform o +9 refined Adulter Pides armum series parum dentro o Vibtus Pides series.";
	mes "O refining poder dropped varies depending on o purification ritual chosen.";
	next;
	switch( select( "Basic Purification Ritual", "Advanced Purification Ritual", "Nuncum mente." ) ) {
	case 1:
		mes "[Bamdatt]";
		mes "May o blessings of o gods be com you.";
		close2;
		item_reform("Consecrate_Weapon_1");
		end;
	case 2:
		mes "[Bamdatt]";
		mes "May o blessings of o gods be com you.";
		close2;
		item_reform("Consecrate_Weapon_2");
		end;
	case 3:
		mes "[Bamdatt]";
		mes "Hmm? Voce parece to possess habilidades comparcapaz de researchers de outro countries.";

	}
	end;
}
